TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于性能度量的框架，用于证明二元动作、实区间状态空间的Restless多臂老虎机问题的阈值可索引性并计算Whittle指数。该框架扩展了折扣部分守恒律(PCL)方法，通过边际资源正性和边际分部积分恒等式，将阈值可索引性等价于边际生产率(MP)指数的单调性和连续性，并成功应用于网络爬虫、噪声信道传输和标量卡尔曼滤波老虎机等场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Whittle索引策略广泛应用于Restless多臂老虎机问题，但其应用需要证明可索引性和计算Whittle指数，这两项任务在分析上具有挑战性。现有方法通常需要先证明阈值最优性再证明最优阈值关于资源价格的单调性，过程繁琐。本文旨在开发一个更直接的框架，绕过这些中间步骤，直接从阈值策略的奖励和资源度量出发，简化可索引性的验证和Whittle指数的计算。

Method: 1. 提出性能度量框架，扩展折扣PCL方法到与准则无关的设置；2. 引入边际资源正性和边际分部积分恒等式两个关键条件；3. 建立主定理：在上述条件下，阈值可索引性等价于MP指数的单调性和连续性，且MP指数等于Whittle指数；4. 通过消失折扣技术将框架特化到离散时间长期平均准则；5. 通过连续延拓或消失折扣极限处理异常状态（边际资源分母为零的情况）。

Result: 1. 恢复了网络爬虫和噪声信道传输问题的已知长期平均Whittle指数；2. 对于标量卡尔曼滤波老虎机，证明了正则部分平均成本结果，将剩余的可索引性问题归结为显式的异常状态度量极限猜想。

Conclusion: 本文提出的性能度量框架为证明阈值可索引性和计算Whittle指数提供了一种通用、简洁的方法，避免了传统路径中先证明阈值最优性再证明单调性的繁琐步骤，并通过多个应用案例验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Restless multiarmed bandits are Markov decision process models for allocating a scarce resource among projects whose states evolve under active or passive actions. Whittle's index policy is widely used for such problems, but its application to a given model requires both a proof of indexability and a means of computing the index, two analytically challenging tasks. This paper develops a performance-metric framework for proving threshold-indexability and computing Whittle indices for binary-action projects with real interval state spaces. The framework extends discounted partial conservation law \textup{(PCL)} methods to a criterion-agnostic setting and works directly with reward and resource metrics of threshold policies, rather than first proving threshold optimality and then monotonicity of optimal thresholds in the resource price. The main theorem is a verification and characterization result: under marginal-resource positivity and a marginal integration-by-parts identity, threshold-indexability is equivalent to monotonicity and continuity of the marginal productivity (MP) index, which then equals the Whittle index. The framework is specialized to the discrete-time long-run average criterion by a vanishing-discount transfer of discounted threshold metrics and includes exceptional states where the MP marginal-resource denominator vanishes, handled by continuous extension or vanishing-discount limits. Applications to web crawling and noisy-channel transmission recover known long-run average Whittle indices. For scalar Kalman-filter bandits, it proves a regular-part average-cost result and reduces the remaining indexability question to explicit exceptional-state metric-limit conjectures.

</details>


### [86] [Major-Minor LQ Mean Field Games with Erroneous Initial Information: Distributed Error Estimation and Strategy Modification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22558)
*Yuxin Jin,Haotian Wang,Wang Yao,Xiao Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有错误初始信息和受限观测结构的主从线性二次平均场博弈，提出了基于最大似然估计的分布式误差识别方法和策略修正方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际主从平均场博弈中，初始信息往往存在误差，且每个智能体无法直接观测到平均场状态，需要设计方法识别并修正这些误差。

Method: 将误差识别建模为参数估计问题，利用离散时间局部观测构造最大似然估计器；在中间时刻基于估计误差重构当前平均场并切换相应控制律。

Result: 初始信息误差通过博弈动力学线性传播，导致主智能体状态、实际平均场和内部更新平均场产生显式偏差；主智能体的估计精度取决于观测到的从智能体数量而非其身份。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够有效识别并修正初始误差，数值结果验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: This paper studies major-minor linear-quadratic mean field games (MMLQMFGs) with erroneous initial information under a constrained observation structure. Each minor agent observes only its own state and the major agent's state, while the major agent observes its own state and the states of a subset of minor agents; neither side observes the mean field state directly. We show that the initial-information errors propagate linearly through the game dynamics and lead to explicit deviations in the major state, the actual mean field, and the agents' internally updated mean field states. Based on this structure, we formulate distributed error identification as a parameter-estimation problem from discrete-time local observations and construct maximum-likelihood estimators for unknown initial errors. We then propose an estimate-based strategy modification at an intermediate time by reconstructing the current mean field from the estimated errors and switching to the corresponding control law. We also characterize the resulting estimation errors and show that, in the present symmetric setting, the major agent's estimation precision depends on the number of observed minor agents but not on their identities. Numerical results illustrate the proposed method.

</details>

Method: SEGOMOE采用自适应高斯过程模型处理混合设计变量，通过组合多个专家模型（expert models）来应对目标和约束中的非线性问题，基于开源替代建模工具箱（SMT）实现，支持多保真数据，并利用高维分解策略处理复杂优化问题。

Result: 通过标准基准测试和真实航空航天应用验证，SEGOMOE在多种优化场景（单/多目标、隐式约束、高维分解）中表现鲁棒且高效，证明了其应对多学科挑战的通用能力。

Conclusion: SEGOMOE是一种有效且通用的贝叶斯优化工具，特别适用于处理航空航天领域计算昂贵且含混合变量的复杂系统优化问题，具有良好的实用价值和扩展潜力。

Abstract: This study introduces SEGOMOE, a Bayesian optimization tool for optimizing complex, computationally expensive systems, especially in aeronautics. It efficiently handles mixed design variables (continuous, discrete, categorical, hierarchical) using adaptive Gaussian process models. SEGOMOE combines expert models to address nonlinearities in objectives and constraints, leveraging the open-source Surrogate Modeling Toolbox (SMT). The tool supports multi-fidelity data and solves both single- and multi-objective problems, including hidden constraints and high-dimensional decomposition. Validated through benchmarks and real-world aeronautical applications, SEGOMOE proves to be robust and versatile for tackling multidisciplinary challenges.

</details>


### [88] [A Formal Kinetic Theory for Zeroth-Order Newton Dynamics:Stein-Corrected Hessian Estimation and Curvature--Variance Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22567)
*Shihao Ji,Mingyu Li,Zihui Song*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为黑箱优化中的零阶牛顿类方法建立了动力学框架，揭示了有偏海森估计的Gaussian-Stein校正机制，推导了逆海森线性化下的双噪声通道（梯度噪声和海森噪声），建立了小质量动理学极限下的Lyapunov界，并展示了曲率-方差权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当梯度和海森矩阵均不可用（黑箱优化）时，零阶牛顿方法的行为与一阶无梯度方法存在本质差异，现有理论缺乏对其动力学行为的系统理解。

Method: 1) 提出基于动理学框架的统一建模；2) 发现随机方向海森估计存在偏差，引入Gaussian-Stein校正；3) 对逆海森进行线性化，识别梯度噪声和海森噪声两个通道；4) 通过小质量动理学极限将有限步牛顿更新与欠阻尼相空间模型关联；5) 过阻尼空间极限导出Lyapunov界，揭示曲率-方差权衡。

Result: 推导出海森噪声通道的二次差分因子μ_H^{-4}；建立步长、批大小、平滑半径和正则化之间的曲率-方差权衡关系；数值实验验证了估计量恒等式、梯度与海森方差律、维度缩放、逆扰动精度及优化行为。

Conclusion: 零阶牛顿方法可被系统地理解为一个受控的动力学系统，其性能受梯度噪声和海森噪声的双重影响，通过Gaussian-Stein校正可消除估计偏差，曲率-方差权衡为算法参数选择提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Zeroth-order Newton-type methods are useful when gradients and Hessians are unavailable, but they behave quite differently from first-order gradient-free methods. We develop a kinetic framework for algorithms that estimate both gradient and Hessian from black-box function values. The naive random-direction Hessian estimator turns out to be biased even on quadratics; a Gaussian--Stein correction is needed to estimate the Hessian of the Gaussian-smoothed objective. Linearizing the inverse Hessian exposes two noise channels: gradient noise preconditioned by the inverse Hessian, and Hessian noise transmitted through an inverse-Hessian sandwich. Under a noisy oracle the second channel carries the second-difference factor $μ_H^{-4}$. A small-mass kinetic lift links the finite-step Newton update to an underdamped phase-space model; the overdamped spatial limit yields a Lyapunov bound that exposes the curvature--variance trade-off between step size, batch sizes, smoothing radii, and regularization. Numerical experiments confirm estimator identities, the gradient and Hessian variance laws, dimension scaling, inverse-perturbation accuracy, and optimization behavior under query-budget and regularization ablations.

</details>


### [89] [Rational Minimax Approximations for Matrix-Valued Functions: Existence, Optimality and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22576)
*Lei-Hong Zhang,Chenkun Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究连续复矩阵值函数在Frobenius范数下的有理极小极大逼近，其中所有逼近项共享共同分母。证明了点集上矩阵值逼近的存在性，建立了局部/全局极小极大逼近的Kolmogorov对偶准则和Ruttan型充分最优性条件，并建立了连续、边界和离散逼近之间的联系，为计算和验证矩阵值有理极小极大逼近提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的标量有理逼近推广到矩阵值情形，解决系统建模、微波设计和非线性特征值问题中矩阵值函数逼近的实际需求。

Method: 通过推广Walsh的标量结果证明存在性；建立矩阵值Kolmogorov对偶准则和Ruttan型充分最优性条件；利用最大模原理建立连续、边界与离散逼近之间的联系。

Result: 证明了点集上矩阵值有理极小极大逼近的存在性；给出了局部和全局最优性的充要条件；揭示了当原始问题有解时强对偶性与Ruttan充分最优性条件的等价性，且m-d-Lawson迭代的最优性方程与Kolmogorov对偶准则一致。

Conclusion: 为矩阵值有理极小极大逼近的认证和计算提供了完整的理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we study rational minimax approximation for continuous complex matrix-valued functions in the Frobenius norm, where all approximant entries share a common denominator. This generalizes classical scalar rational approximation, with applications in system modeling, microwave design, and nonlinear eigenvalue problems. We first prove the existence of such matrix-valued approximants on point sets dense in themselves, extending Walsh's foundational scalar result. Next, we establish characterizations of the local and global minimax approximants by deriving primal/dual matrix-valued Kolmogorov criteria and a Ruttan-type sufficient condition for global optimality. For analytic functions on a continuum, we link continuum minimax approximation to approximation on its boundary, and finite boundary samples via the maximum norm principle. We show that Ruttan's sufficient optimality condition provides a certificate under which a minimax approximant obtained from the boundary or from a discrete set of boundary nodes also solves the original continuum problem. Finally, for discrete approximation, we connect these conditions to a dual problem and the related dual-based numerical method m-d-Lawson: when the original minimax problem admits a solution, strong duality is equivalent to Ruttan's sufficient optimality condition, and, the optimality equations underlying the m-d-Lawson iteration coincide with Kolmogorov's dual criteria. These results provide a theoretical basis for certifying and computing matrix-valued rational minimax approximants.

</details>


### [90] [A unified approach to non-unique fixed points via modified C-class Ciric-type contractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22579)
*Olaoluwa Omidire,Memudu Olatinwo,Kamiludeen Tijani,Babatunde Ishola*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过引入改进的C类函数，在完备度量空间中为Ciric型映射建立了新的非唯一不动点定理，无需严格压缩性和全局Lipschitz条件，并提供了唯一性判定标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典不动点理论通常要求严格压缩性并保证唯一性，限制了在本质非唯一不动点映射上的应用。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 引入改进的三变量C类函数与改变距离函数结合，构造广义压缩不等式；在轨道连续性和轨道完备性条件下，证明Picard迭代收敛。

Result: 建立了非唯一不动点存在性定理；给出了唯一性判据；通过精心设计的算例验证了框架的尖锐性和普适性，严格推广了Ciric、Chatterjea压缩及混合(Φ,ψ)-压缩。

Conclusion: 该统一框架为非线性算子理论、迭代方法及优化与变分问题中的应用提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Fixed point theory is central to nonlinear analysis, yet classical results often assume strict contractivity and guarantee uniqueness, limiting applicability to mappings with inherently non-unique fixed points. This paper addresses this gap by introducing a modified C-class function and establishing new non-unique fixed point theorems for Ciric-type mappings in complete metric spaces. We construct a generalized contractive inequality using a three-variable modifed C-class function combined with an altering distance function. Under orbital continuity and orbital completeness, we prove that Picard iteration converges to a fixed point without requiring global Lipschitz conditions or strict contractivity. Carefully designed examples illustrate the sharpness and generality of the framework, strictly extending classical Ciric and Chatterjea contractions as well as hybrid $(Φ,ψ)$-contractions. Our main contributions are: (i) a unified framework encompassing classical and generalized C-class contractions, (ii) existence results for non-unique fixed points, (iii) a simple criterion for uniqueness when desired, and (iv) practical validation through nontrivial examples. These results provide a robust foundation for further research in nonlinear operator theory, iterative methods, and applications in optimization and variational problems.

</details>


### [91] [Optimality of a Threshold Policy for a Queueing System with One Fast Server and Two Identical Slow Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22580)
*Weina Wang,Taha Ameen,Yudong Chen,Yige Hong,Josh Nichols,Matthew Zurek*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究具有三个服务器（一个快速服务器和两个相同慢速服务器）的排队系统的最优控制问题，证明了阈值策略在该系统中的最优性。文中披露核心技术思想由GPT-5.5 Pro生成，作者验证了证明并用Lean 4验证了三个关键引理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Lin和Kumar（1984）提出的双服务器（一快一慢）排队系统最优阈值策略推广到三服务器（一快两慢）场景，这是该问题长期未解决的开放难题。

Method: 采用GPT-5.5 Pro生成核心技术思路，作者验证并改写证明以保证严谨性和清晰度，同时使用Lean 4形式化验证了三个关键引理。

Conclusion: 成功将双服务器阈值策略最优性结果推广到三服务器场景，为该方向更一般性的结果奠定了基础。

Abstract: This paper studies the optimal control problem of a queueing system with three servers: one fast server and two identical slow servers. The two-server version of this problem, with one fast server and one slow server, was introduced by Lin and Kumar (1984), and the optimal policy has been shown to be of threshold type. However, generalizing this result beyond the two-server setting has been considered an open problem. In this paper, we resolve the first nontrivial case by proving that a threshold policy is optimal for the three-server system considered.
  The core technical ideas in this paper are generated by GPT-5.5 Pro. We have included a short report describing the authors' interactions with GPT-5.5 Pro. The authors verified the proofs and rewrote the paper for better rigor, clarity, and exposition. In addition, three key lemmas have also been verified in Lean 4.

</details>


### [92] [Randomized Constructive Heuristics for the VRPTW: A Focus on Regret-k](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22581)
*Florian Rascoussier,Romain Billot,Lina Fahed,Christine Solnon*

TL;DR: 本文研究了三种经典构造式启发式算法（最近邻、最优插入和Regret-k）在随机化多起点框架下求解带时间窗车辆路径问题(VRPTW)的效果，提出了一种新颖的Regret-k随机化方法，分离"谁"（插入哪个客户）和"哪里"（插入位置）的决策，并分析了与局部搜索和蚁群优化(ACO)结合的性能，实验在Solomon和Gehring & Homberger基准集（100-1000客户）上验证。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出一种将Regret-k的"谁"和"哪里"决策分离的随机化方法，对客户按遗憾值比例进行概率选择以保留前瞻性同时增加多样性；并结合局部搜索和ACO进行性能提升；所有组件用现代C++实现并通过pybind11绑定到Python。

Result: 在Solomon和Gehring & Homberger基准集（100-1000客户）上，遵循DIMACS 2021约定，与已知最优解和Hybrid Genetic Search求解器对比，分析了解决方案质量与计算成本之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 该研究为MAMUT项目中面向时间依赖VRPTW的混合方法（ACO、ILS、Branch-and-Price）提供了初步基础。

Abstract: The Vehicle Routing Problem with Time Windows (VRPTW) requires a fleet of capacitated vehicles to serve customers within strict time windows while minimizing total travel time. Constructive heuristics are fundamental for generating solutions and commonly serve as starting points for metaheuristics such as Iterated Local Search (ILS), Genetic Algorithms, and Ant Colony Optimization (ACO). This work studies three classical constructive heuristics - Nearest Neighbor, Best Insertion, and Regret-k - within a randomized, multi-start framework, a setting that remains largely under-explored despite their well-established deterministic use. We address how to effectively randomize these heuristics and, in particular, propose a novel randomization of Regret-k by separating the ''who'' (which customer to insert next) and ''where'' (insertion position) decisions, applying a probabilistic selection proportional to the regret value to preserve the heuristic's foresight while injecting the diversity needed for a multi-start approach. We further study the impact of combining these greedy randomized constructions with Local Search and ACO. Experiments follow the DIMACS 2021 conventions (integer, truncated Euclidean distances) on the Solomon and Gehring \& Homberger benchmarks (100--1000 customers), comparing against the Best-Known Solutions and the state-of-the-art Hybrid Genetic Search solver, with all critical components implemented in modern C++ and bound to Python via pybind11. To the best of our knowledge, the randomization of Regret-k has never been examined; our analysis highlights the trade-off between solution quality and computational cost, providing a preliminary step toward hybrid methods (ACO, ILS, Branch-and-Price) for the Time-Dependent VRPTW within the MAMUT project.

</details>

Abstract: In urban contexts, travel times vary strongly with the time of day and traffic conditions. The Time-Dependent Vehicle Routing Problem with Time Windows (TDVRPTW) extends the classical VRPTW by making travel times depend on departure time, with the objective of minimizing the total travel time of at most $k$ vehicle routes serving customers within strict time windows. Exact resolution of this realistic yet understudied problem currently relies on the Branch \& Price approach of Dabia et al. (2013), in which a Column Generation scheme decomposes the problem into a set-partitioning master problem and a resource-constrained shortest-path pricing problem solved by dynamic programming. This work, conducted within the MAMUT project (Machine Learning and Matheuristics for Urban Transport), aims to advance the state of the art for the exact and explainable resolution of the TDVRPTW. We propose to reimplement and revisit the pioneering Branch \& Price algorithm by transferring recent advances made on the single-vehicle variant (TDTSPTW) to the multi-vehicle setting, notably an exact anytime extension of A* and a Large Neighborhood Search guided by dynamic programming for the pricing sub-problem. We further explore the integration of data mining and machine learning to guide column generation by exploiting knowledge from previously computed routes. The goal is a high-performance and interpretable solver that meets the requirements of real-world urban transport while preserving guarantees on solution quality.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典最优传输的线性目标函数无法保证菱形运输的唯一极小性，因此需要拓展到非凸、非经典的最优传输框架（如QOT和DOT）来研究菱形运输族的最优化性质。

Method: 在QOT框架下，针对对称一维边缘分布，分析成本函数依赖于坐标内距离的QOT问题；在DOT框架下，研究自然成本类中的菱形型运输；并识别DOT与QOT的交集（即二次型扭曲函数）。

Result: 菱形运输在QOT框架下是大量成本函数的优化器（包括正定凸性条件下的乘积成本和混合矩形成本），在矩形成本下是唯一极小元（边界情况除外）；在DOT框架下，菱形型运输是自然成本类的极小元，菱形运输在特例中唯一。

Conclusion: 菱形运输在非经典OT框架（QOT和DOT）中具有重要的最优化地位，拓展了最优传输理论在非凸成本函数下的应用。

Abstract: The diamond transport is generated by the uniform law on a diamond-shaped copula support. Since a classical optimal transport (OT) objective is affine in the coupling, this transport cannot be the unique minimizer in the classical setting. We study a broader family of transports, called diamond-type transports, in non-classical settings such as quadratic-form optimal transport (QOT) and distorted optimal transport (DOT), which are generally nonconvex. Our main results are within the QOT framework: for symmetric one-dimensional marginals, the diamond transport is an optimizer for a large class of QOT problems whose costs depend on within-coordinate distances. Examples include product costs under positive-definiteness and convexity conditions and, in particular, mixed rectangular costs. For rectangular costs, we show that the diamond transport is the unique minimizer except for boundary cases. In the DOT framework, diamond-type transports are minimizers for a natural class of cost, and the diamond transport is the unique minimizer in specialized examples. We also identify the intersection between DOT and QOT, which corresponds precisely to quadratic distortion functions.

</details>

*Yunhao Yao,Siyu Jing,Yang Yang,Qiang Xu,Changqi Weng,Xiang-Yang Li*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有需求响应方法要么仅优化电费或用户舒适度而忽略系统级平衡，要么忽视异构能源实体的多样动态消费模式导致过度或不足调节，亟需一种兼顾系统平衡、经济效益和可再生能源消纳的灵活负荷调节方法。

Method: DRP-FLR框架包含三步：1）通过嵌入外部知识（实体信息、预测时间）到历史负荷表示中实现精准短期负荷预测；2）通过聚类历史负荷曲线构建实体特定的负荷模式画像，并匹配预测负荷与模式画像来估计DR潜力；3）将灵活负荷调节建模为混合整数优化问题，用MILP求解器联合优化DR利用率、参与者经济效益和可再生能源消纳，同时保证供需平衡和经济可行性。

Result: 在区域电网和校园微电网实验中，DRP-FLR将调节偏差降低36.63%-91.87%，参与者收益平均提升44.66%。

Conclusion: DRP-FLR通过知识增强预测、实体特定负荷画像和联合优化，有效解决了现有需求响应方法在系统级平衡和异构实体适应性方面的不足，显著提升了调节精度和经济效益。

Abstract: The rapid growth of AI workloads and renewable energy resources exacerbates supply-demand imbalance in power systems, making traditional load regulation designed for efficient allocation inadequate and motivating demand response (DR) mechanisms to enable load controllability in smart grids. However, existing DR-oriented approaches either focus on optimizing electricity cost or occupant comfort with limited benefit to system-level balance. Others overlook the diverse and dynamic consumption patterns of heterogeneous energy entities, leading to significant over- or under-regulation. Therefore, we propose DRP-FLR. First, DRP-FLR achieves accurate short-term load forecasting by embedding exogenous knowledge (e.g., entity information, prediction time) into historical load representations. Next, it constructs entity-specific load-pattern profiles by clustering historical load curves, and estimates DR potential by matching forecasted loads with pattern profiles. Finally, DRP-FLR formulates flexible load regulation as a mixed-integer optimization problem and solves it with an MILP solver to jointly optimize DR utilization, participant economic benefit, and renewable accommodation, while enforcing supply-demand balance and economic feasibility. Experiments on a regional grid and a campus microgrid show that DRP-FLR reduces regulation deviation by 36.63%-91.87% and improves participant benefit by 44.66% on average.

</details>

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了d'Alembert泛函方程在正实数路径上诱导的动力学作用量，证明了其强凸性。通过校准的d'Alembert力得到cosh代价函数，在对数坐标下作用量具有强凸性，进而导出了全局极小性的一侧弦条件、唯一均匀对数速度极小化子、精确Bregman/Pythagorean恒等式等结论。该定理是纯数学的，其与牛顿力学和快度力学的桥梁是有条件的，需要额外的运动学嵌入、质量耦合、时间校准和Hamiltonian-primary Legendre结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究d'Alembert泛函方程在正路径上诱导的动力学作用量的凸性性质，并探索其与牛顿力学和相对论力学的联系与区别。

Method: 通过对数坐标变换将代价函数转化为cosh形式，利用几何插值（对数空间插值）证明作用量的强凸性，进而推导全局极小性的弦条件、唯一极小化子以及Bregman/Pythagorean恒等式；同时讨论该数学结构与物理力学（牛顿力学、相对论力学）建立联系所需的条件。

Result: 证明了cosh作用量在对数速度坐标下的强凸性；得到了表征全局极小性的一侧弦条件；证明了固定端点下唯一极小化子为均匀对数速度路径；导出了精确的作用量缺口Bregman恒等式及Friedrichs-Poincaré定量界；澄清了该cosh作用量与狭义相对论自由粒子Hamiltonian的差异（轮廓一致但Hamiltonian不同）。

Conclusion: 该数学结构具有丰富的凸分析性质，但与物理力学的桥接需要额外的结构条件，且一旦加入非仿射严格凸势能项，联合凸性即被破坏，回到经典平稳作用量图景。

Abstract: We study the kinetic action that d'Alembert's functional equation induces on positive paths in $\Rplus$, and prove it strongly convex. Calibrated d'Alembert forces the cosh cost $\Jcost(x)=\tfrac12(x+x^{-1})-1$, i.e.\ $\Jlog(ξ)=\coshξ-1$ in the log coordinate $ξ=\log x$. Evaluating this log-cost at the log-\emph{velocity} $\dotξ$ rather than the log-position -- a single postulate (Postulate~\ref{post:step}) -- yields $\actionA[γ]=\int_a^b(\cosh\dotξ-1)\,dt$, strongly convex under geometric (log-space) interpolation. This convexity has three consequences, none requiring an Euler--Lagrange equation, a Fréchet derivative, or a second variation. First, a one-sided chord condition characterizes global minimality. Second, the unique fixed-endpoint minimizer is the uniform-log-velocity path. Third, the action gap obeys an exact Bregman / Pythagorean identity $\actionA[γ]-\actionA[γ_*]=\int D_\Kkin(\dotξ\,\|\,\dotξ_*)\,dt$, sharpened by a quantitative Friedrichs--Poincaré bound on $\log(γ/γ_*)$. It has a dually-flat / Hessian-manifold reading in the additive coordinate $ξ$. \\ This theorem is purely mathematical, and we delimit it. The bridge to Newtonian and rapidity mechanics is \emph{conditional}, requiring structure beyond Postulate~\ref{post:step}: a kinematic embedding, a mass coupling, a time calibration, and a Hamiltonian-primary Legendre structure. Granted these, the cosh action recovers the Newtonian small-step limit and the rapidity profile $\Kkin_m(φ)=m(γ_L-1)$; yet the cosh-dual Hamiltonian is \emph{not} the special-relativistic free-particle Hamiltonian (Proposition~\ref{prop:not-SR}), the agreement being one of profile, not an identity of Hamiltonians. Global minimality is a free-sector phenomenon: once a non-affine strictly convex potential is added, joint convexity is lost and the classical stationary-action picture returns.

</details>


### [97] [The Combined Van-and-Mopeds Routing Problem with Time Windows: Formulation, Variants, and Solution Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22601)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧洲密集城市中，货车难以进入或效率低下的区域催生了对"货车+摩托车"联合配送模式的需求，现有研究缺乏对该协同机制的正式建模与求解方法。

Method: 提出22个结构化约束族的MILP公式；设计三种操作变体（标准模型、主动等待货车变体、公共车场变体）；采用字典序两阶段优化框架；开发三种图稀疏化预处理技术（减少最多25%变量和约束）；提出基于聚类的联合路由（CBCR）启发式算法。

Result: MILP可在一小时内求解最多48个客户的实例；CBCR启发式在有利条件下可将可解规模扩展到最多80个客户。

Conclusion: 所提出的CVMRPTW模型和算法能有效解决欧洲城市最后一公里配送中的货车-摩托车协同路由问题，在中等规模实例上兼具精确性和可扩展性。

Abstract: We introduce and study the Combined Van-and-Mopeds Routing Problem with Time Windows (CVMRPTW), a novel variant of the heterogeneous fleet vehicle routing problem motivated by last-mile parcel delivery in dense European cities. In the CVMRPTW, a single van serves as a mobile base from which on-demand mopeds are deployed to reach customers located in areas inaccessible or inefficient for vans. The primary objective is to minimise the number of mopeds used while guaranteeing that all delivery time windows are met. We propose a Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) formulation that captures vehicle synchronisation, capacity tracking, and time-window compliance through 22 structured constraint families, and extend it to three operationally motivated variants: the standard model, the active-waiting-van variant, and the common-depot variant. A lexicographic two-stage optimisation framework allows secondary objectives-route duration, combined travel time, and combined distance-to be optimised without sacrificing the primary objective. Three graph-sparsification preprocessing techniques reduce the number of variables and constraints by up to 25%. For instances beyond the practical limits of exact solvers, we develop the Cluster-Based Combined Routing (CBCR) heuristic, which decomposes the problem into a clustering phase, a cluster-level exact solve, and a route-reconstruction phase. Computational experiments on real-world instances derived from OpenStreetMap data for Lisbon and Stuttgart show that the MILP solves instances with up to 48 customers to feasibility within one hour, and that the CBCR heuristic extends tractability to instances with up to 80 customers under favourable conditions.

</details>


### [98] [Transform Method for Stochastic Processing and Matching Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22618)
*Sushil Mahavir Varma,Prakirt Jhunjhunwala,Daniela Hurtado-Lange,Siva Theja Maguluri*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了变换方法（Transform Method）作为一种统一且易处理的分析框架，用于随机处理与匹配网络（SPNs/SMNs）的稳态分析。该方法通过直接处理预极限系统，利用指数测试函数的零漂移性质推导出队列长度分布的变换函数显式方程，避免了传统过程级收敛方法的技术难题，为非渐近性能保证和实际系统设计提供了有力工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代服务系统（如云数据中心、网约车平台）在大规模运行中面临严重的拥塞挑战。传统的过程级收敛方法（流体极限和扩散极限）技术密集且对有限规模的实际系统指导有限，需要一种统一、易处理且能提供非渐近性能保证的分析框架。

Method: 变换方法通过直接处理预极限系统，利用指数测试函数的零漂移性质，推导出队列长度分布的变换函数（如矩母函数）的显式函数方程，作为全局平衡方程的替代。该方法自2020年提出后，已扩展至客户放弃、状态相关到达、马尔可夫调制到达、大规模系统和多瓶颈多维网络等复杂场景。

Result: 该方法提供了尖锐的非渐近性能保证，弥合了理论渐近分析与实际系统行为之间的差距，并已在匹配市场、网络化服务系统等多个领域证明了其实用相关性。

Conclusion: 变换方法提供了直接关联系统参数的可解释界，是系统设计与控制中替代纯仿真或渐近分析方法的强大分析工具，具有重要的理论价值和实际应用前景。

Abstract: Modern service systems -- ranging from cloud data centers to ride-hailing platforms -- operate at massive scales where congestion is a critical challenge. Utilizing an operations research approach, these systems are analyzed by modeling them as complex stochastic processes, which are typically understood through process-level convergence to fluid and diffusion limits. However, these methods often prove technically dense and provide limited guidance for finite, practical system scales. The transform method, presented in this tutorial, was recently developed as a unified and tractable framework for the steady-state analysis of Stochastic Processing and Matching Networks (SPNs/SMNs). The transform method overcomes the technical hurdles of process-level convergence by working directly with the pre-limit system. By exploiting the zero-drift property of exponential test functions, the method derives explicit functional equations (acting as a proxy for global balance equations) for the transforms (such as moment-generating functions) of queue-length distributions. This approach provides sharp, non-asymptotic performance guarantees, bridging the gap between theoretical asymptotics and real-world system behavior.
  Since its introduction in 2020 for load-balancing in data center networks, the transform method has been extended to handle realistic complexities, including customer abandonment, state-dependent arrivals, Markov-modulated arrivals, large-system scale, and multi-dimensional networks with multiple bottlenecks. We survey these theoretical advances and demonstrate their practical relevance across diverse domains, such as matching markets and networked service systems. The transform method provides interpretable bounds tied directly to system parameters, offering a powerful analytical alternative to simulation-heavy or purely asymptotic approaches for system design and control.

</details>


### [99] [A Resolution of the SS--RS--GD Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22620)
*Binghui Peng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文解决了Yun等人提出的SS-RS-GD范数不等式猜想：SS-RS不等式不成立（n=3, K=2, d=4时存在反例），而RS-GD不等式在适当条件下成立，证明由GPT-5.5 Pro辅助完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Yun, Sra和Jadbabaie在COLT 2021公开提出的关于单次洗牌SGD、随机重排SGD和梯度下降期望迭代算子范数之间的不等式猜想。

Method: 针对SS-RS不等式，构造了n=3, K=2, d=4条件下条件数任意接近1的正半定矩阵反例；针对RS-GD不等式，利用对称矩阵满足(1-1/(4n²+1))I ≼ A_i ≼ I的条件进行理论证明，证明过程借助GPT-5.5 Pro扩展提示生成。

Result: SS-RS不等式被证伪：存在条件数任意接近1的PSD矩阵使得∥W_ss∥ > ∥W_rs∥；RS-GD不等式被证明成立：对所有满足条件的对称矩阵有∥W_rs∥ ≤ ∥W_gd∥。

Conclusion: Yun等人的猜想部分正确、部分错误，其中SS-RS不等式的失败表明单次洗牌SGD的收敛性可能比随机重排SGD更差，而RS-GD不等式的成立则确认了随机重排SGD的期望迭代范数不超过梯度下降。

Abstract: Yun, Sra, and Jadbabaie (COLT 2021, open question) conjectured the SS--RS--GD inequalities: for well-conditioned symmetric matrices $A_1,\dots,A_n$, the operators $W_{ss}$, $W_{rs}$, and $W_{gd}$ that encode the expected iterate of single-shuffle SGD, random-reshuffle SGD, and gradient descent on a quadratic finite sum should satisfy \[
  \|W_{ss}\|\le \| W_{rs}\|\le \|W_{gd}\|. \] The conjecture is resolved,
  $\bullet$ SS-RS inequality fails. Already for $n=3$, $K=2$, and $d=4$, we exhibit explicit PSD matrices whose condition number is arbitrarily close to $1$, yet $\|W_{ss}\|>\|W_{rs}\|$.
  $\bullet$ RS-GD inequality holds. For every symmetric $A_i$ with $\bigl(1-\frac1{4n^2+1}\bigr)I\preceq A_i\preceq I$, one has $\|W_{rs}\|\le\|W_{gd}\|$.
  The proof was found via GPT-5.5 Pro extended prompted by the author.

</details>


### [100] [Resilience Enhancement of Electricity-Computing Interdependency System against Contingency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22623)
*Minqiao Zheng,Zejun Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对AI时代电力与算力高度耦合但常被低估的问题，提出了电算互依系统（ECIS），将数据中心建模为电力转算力的设施，并设计了考虑电力约束、算力供给和基础设施互依性的恢复优化策略。案例表明，该策略能根据系统状态动态调整数据中心电力配额，在电力短缺期间将系统韧性提升20%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力和算力高度互依，尤其在AI革命背景下，但这种依赖性在正常和恢复运行中常被低估，缺乏有效的量化与协同恢复机制。

Method: 构建电算互依系统（ECIS），将数据中心建模为电力转算力的设施；提出考虑电力约束、算力供给和基础设施互依性的恢复优化策略。

Result: 所提策略能根据系统状态动态调整数据中心电力配额，在电力短缺期间将系统韧性提升20%以上。

Conclusion: 通过ECIS框架和动态配额调整，有效改善了电算互依系统的恢复性能，显著提升了系统韧性。

Abstract: Electrical power and computing power are highly interdependent especially due to the AI revolution. This dependency is largely underestimated during normal and recovery operation. To address this issue, an Electricity-Computing Interdependency System (ECIS) is developed in this paper, which first quantifies the interdependencies between ECIS and other critical infrastructures. The data center (DC) is modeled as a facility that transforms electrical power into computing power, and a recovery optimization strategy for ECIS is then proposed considering electrical power constraints, computing power supply, and infrastructure interdependencies. The case study demonstrates that the proposed strategy dynamically adjusts the DC electrical power quota according to system states, improving system resilience by more than 20% during power shortage.

</details>


### [101] [Spectral Analysis of Heavy-Ball Q-value Iteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22627)
*Donghwan Lee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究带动量的Q值迭代（重球QVI）的收敛性与加速效果，利用切换线性系统理论和联合谱半径进行分析，并探讨其比标准QVI更快的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然值迭代的加速已被广泛研究，但针对控制任务中重球QVI加速的工作较少。

Method: 将重球QVI转化为精确的切换线性系统，利用联合谱半径解释其收敛性，并基于JSR术语研究重球QVI快于标准QVI的条件。

Result: 从切换线性系统和联合谱半径的角度成功分析了重球QVI的收敛性，并给出了其加速的条件。

Conclusion: 重球QVI在某些条件下可以比标准QVI更快收敛，SLS和JSR是分析该方法的有效工具。

Abstract: We study the convergence and acceleration of Q-value iteration (QVI) with momentum, or heavy-ball QVI. Although acceleration of value iteration has been studied extensively, there has been less work on the acceleration of heavy-ball QVI for control tasks. We analyze heavy-ball QVI from the viewpoint of switched linear system (SLS) theory and the joint spectral radius (JSR). First, we convert heavy-ball QVI into an exact SLS and interpret its convergence through the JSR. We also study, in JSR-based terms, conditions under which heavy-ball QVI can be faster than standard QVI.

</details>


### [102] [Network analysis reveals phase transitions in agro-food nitrogen systems with contrasting feeding capacity and land requirement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22628)
*Adrien Fauste-Gay,Jeremie Unterberger,Olivier Vidal,Lauriane Mouysset*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个基于网络的建模框架，用于表征农业食品氮系统的替代稳态构型，并识别这些构型之间的相变。模型将生产、分配、回收和损失表示为质量守恒流，通过粗粒度化识别主导回收循环。以法国为案例，分析发现不同的稳态构型被相变分隔，相变对应系统性能指标的变化。传统耕地养活约8.7人/公顷，严格饮食自主需约1970万公顷农业用地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的农业食品转型评估使用优化模型或情景方法，只能返回单一的可行构型，虽然能估计系统性能但提供的关于可行的氮循环构型的信息有限。

Method: 开发了连续时间的区室网络模型，将生产、分配、回收和损失表示为质量守恒流，在稳态条件下分析系统。通过粗粒度化程序识别主导回收循环并推导聚合指标。使用法国参考数据进行参数化，应用于施肥、畜牧和饮食模式案例。

Result: 识别出氮网络的不同稳态构型，由主导回收结构的相变分隔。法国参考系统中，耕地养活能力约8.7人/公顷，严格饮食自主需约1970万公顷农业用地。减少合成施肥而不改变饮食或氮源会导致更高的土地需求或对外部输入的更大依赖。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种映射可行氮系统构型的方法，可在不同参数和约束下分析农业食品氮网络的相变。

Abstract: Agro-food transitions are commonly assessed using optimization models or scenario approaches that return a single feasible configuration. These approaches provide estimates of system performance but limited information on feasible nitrogen cycling configurations. In this study, we develop a network-based modelling framework to characterize alternative steady-state configurations of agro-food nitrogen systems and identify phase transitions between these configurations.
  We formulate a continuous-time compartmental network model in which production, allocation, recycling, and losses are represented as mass-conserving flows. The system is analyzed under steady-state conditions. A coarse-graining procedure identifies dominant recycling cycles and derives aggregate indicators describing system configuration. The model is parameterized using French reference data and applied to case studies covering fertilization, livestock, and dietary patterns.
  The analysis identifies distinct steady-state configurations of the nitrogen network, separated by phase transitions in dominant recycling structures. These transitions correspond to changes in system performance indicators. For the French reference system, cropland-based feeding capacity is about 8.7 people per hectare of cropland (depending on constraints), while strict dietary autonomy requires about 19.7 million hectares of agricultural land. Reducing synthetic fertilization without changes in diet or nitrogen sourcing leads to higher land requirements or greater dependence on external inputs.
  The framework provides a method for mapping feasible nitrogen system configurations and analyzing phase transitions in agro-food nitrogen networks under alternative parameters and constraints.

</details>


### [103] [Modelling the Correlation Structure of Uncertain Input Parameters for Energy System Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22630)
*Klara Maggauer,Yannick Werner,Stefan Strömer,Sonja Wogrin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对能源系统优化模型中忽视输入参数统计依赖性的问题，提出了一个基于copula的工作流来识别、表征和建模线性与单调相关结构，并在奥地利2019-2025年的太阳能发电、日前电价和电力需求数据上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机能源系统优化模型中不确定输入参数之间存在统计依赖性，但常被忽略，这会导致结果显著偏差。为填补这一空白，需要开发一个全面的框架来表征、建模和基准测试统计依赖性。

Method: 提出基于copula的工作流，用于识别、表征和建模输入参数之间的线性与单调相关结构。利用奥地利2019-2025年太阳能发电、日前电价和电力需求数据进行实证演示。

Result: 结果显示这些变量之间存在显著的线性和单调依赖性，且copula方法能够很好地捕捉这些依赖关系。

Conclusion: 该工作流作为全面框架的第一步具有可扩展性，未来可进一步拓展到时变依赖和高维依赖建模。

Abstract: Statistical dependence among uncertain input parameters in stochastic energy system optimization models is often ignored, even though this can substantially bias outcomes. To address this gap, we are developing a comprehensive framework for characterizing, modelling, and benchmarking statistical dependence. In this work, we present a copula-based workflow to identify, characterize, and model linear and monotonic correlation structures between input parameters, representing the first development step towards this framework. We demonstrate our workflow using solar generation, day-ahead electricity prices, and electricity demand data in Austria between 2019 and 2025. Our results show substantial linear and monotonic dependence between these variables, and that this dependence is well captured by the copula-based approach. Finally, building on this scalable foundation, we highlight key levers for next steps towards the full framework, including time-dependent and higher-dimensional dependence modelling.

</details>


### [104] [Mean-field model for pollution abatement via cap and trade mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22638)
*Ofelia Bonesini,Giacomo Lanaro*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个AK生产技术与排放权交易系统(ETS)相结合的均值场模型，研究监管机构如何通过动态拍卖机制最优地控制碳排放许可供给，在减少累积排放与维持总产出之间取得平衡，并利用线性二次型结构和共同噪声，通过耦合FBSDE和Riccati方程刻画了均衡解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在排放权交易系统(ETS)背景下，监管机构需要设计有效的碳排放许可分配机制，以减少累积排放的同时不影响经济总产出。现有研究缺乏对大规模经济体中监管机构最优许可分配政策的系统性刻画。

Method: 采用均值场控制(MFC)框架建模监管机构与竞争性企业之间的互动。企业采用AK生产技术（产出与资本成比例），生产产生排放；监管机构通过动态拍卖机制控制许可供给。利用线性二次型结构及共同噪声，将均衡刻画为耦合的正向-倒向随机微分方程(FBSDE)系统和相关的Riccati方程。

Result: 获得了大规模经济体中最优许可分配政策的可处理解析刻画，通过耦合FBSDE和Riccati方程给出了均衡解的闭式表征。

Conclusion: 该框架为高效排放权交易机制的设计提供了理论洞见，证明了均值场控制方法在环境经济学中政策设计问题的有效性。

Abstract: We consider a mean-field model of competitive firms operating under an AK production technology, where output is proportional to capital and production generates emissions. In this setting, we introduce a regulator whose objective is the reduction of cumulative emissions, in the spirit of Emission Trading Systems (ETS), where firms must hold and trade permits to cover their emissions in a regulated market. The regulator acts as a central planner and controls the supply of permits, balancing emission reduction and aggregate output. Permits are allocated through a dynamic auction mechanism that adjusts supply in real time to achieve both market efficiency and the regulator's long-term goals. The regulator and the firms interact through the endogenous permit price, which is determined by the regulator's policy and affects firms' optimal strategies via the market clearing condition. The regulator's optimal policy is derived within a Mean-Field Control (MFC) framework. Exploiting the linear-quadratic structure and the presence of common noise, we characterise the equilibrium via a system of coupled FBSDEs and associated Riccati equations. Our results provide a tractable characterisation of optimal permit allocation policies in large economies and offer insights into the design of efficient emission trading mechanisms.

</details>


### [105] [Upper and lower approximations of the second order. Exhausters of the second order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22647)
*I. M. Prudnikov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对可微函数定义了二阶上下近似，引入了二阶穷举器（Exhausters），给出了二阶Frèchet次微分与二阶穷举器之间的关系，为Lipschitz函数提供了构造一阶和二阶穷举器的规则，并给出了基于穷举器的极值充要条件，讨论了穷举器在优化中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的一阶优化条件在处理非光滑优化问题时往往信息不足，需要更高效的二阶条件来描述极值行为。本文旨在通过引入二阶穷举器，提供更精细的极值分析工具。

Method: 利用方向可微函数定义了二阶上下近似，并据此引入二阶穷举器的概念；建立了二阶Frèchet次微分与二阶穷举器之间的理论联系；针对Lipschitz函数给出了一阶和二阶穷举器的构造规则；基于穷举器推导出极值的充要条件。

Result: 成功建立了二阶穷举器的理论框架，给出了它与二阶Frèchet次微分的关系，提供了Lipschitz函数穷举器的构造方法，并得到了基于穷举器的极值充要条件。

Conclusion: 二阶穷举器是分析非光滑函数极值问题的有效工具，在优化领域具有重要的应用价值。

Abstract: The upper and lower approximations of the second order are defined for a function differentiable in the directions. Exhausters of the second order are introduced. The relation between the second-order Frechet subdifferentials and the second-order exhausters is given. A rule for constructing exhausters of the first and second orders is given for Lipschitz functions.Necessary and sufficient extremum conditions through exhausters are given. It is discussed about the use of Exhausters in optimization.

</details>


### [106] [Modulus of conically averaged mappings and its applications to angles between two subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22650)
*Honglin Luo,Shuang Song,Xianfu Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了锥平均映射的模数来对锥平均映射进行分类，研究了广义单调映射的单调值和共单调值与其联系，在线性情形下完整刻画了锥平均矩阵并给出了计算模数的显式公式，作为应用计算了子空间之间的Dixmier角和Friedrichs角，并将非线性结果作为线性情形的推广，还研究了hypoconvex函数的近端映射和反射映射的锥平均性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锥平均映射是平均映射的推广，在多种优化算法中具有重要作用，但缺乏对其分类和量化的系统框架，本文旨在通过引入模数来填补这一空白。

Method: 引入锥平均映射的模数进行分类，引入广义单调映射的单调值和共单调值并建立两者联系；在线性情形下完整刻画锥平均矩阵并推导计算模数的显式公式；将线性结果推广到非线性情形，并分析hypoconvex函数的近端和反射映射的锥平均性。

Result: 在线性情形下获得了锥平均矩阵的完整刻画和模数的显式计算公式，成功计算了子空间之间的Dixmier角和Friedrichs角；非线性结果作为线性情形的推广而建立。

Conclusion: 锥平均映射的模数提供了一个有效的分类工具，线性与非线性结果共同构成了锥平均映射的理论框架，对优化算法分析有重要意义。

Abstract: Conically averaged mappings, a generalization of averaged mappings, are important in a wide range of Optimization Algorithms. In this paper, we propose the modulus of conical averagedness to classify conical averaged mappings. Introducing the monotone and comonotone values of generalized monotone mappings, we investigate their connections to the modulus of conical averagedness. In the linear setting, we completely characterize conically averaged matrices, and derive explicit and pleasing formulae for computing their modulus of averagedness. As applications, we compute the Dixmier and Friedrichs angles between two subspaces. Nonlinear results are established as extensions of the linear case. Conical averagedness of proximal and reflection mappings of hypoconvex functions are also studied.

</details>


### [107] [LiFT-MPC: Language-in-the-Loop Feedback Tuning of Cost Previews for MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23832)
*Xinyi Yi,Ioannis Lestas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出LiFT-MPC框架，通过集成语言反馈调优（LiFT）校正方案，在MPC循环中在线更新预测机制，利用控制性能损失函数优化时变目标的预测质量，并在储能管理实验中验证了经济性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有时变目标的模型预测控制中，预测信号（如能源价格）难以仅从历史轨迹准确预测，因为它们依赖于其他上下文事件，需要更有效的预测校正方法。

Method: 提出LiFT-MPC框架，将语言反馈调优（LiFT）校正方案集成到MPC循环中，通过控制性能损失函数在线更新预测机制，并建立了闭环系统的性能保证。

Result: 在基于真实电价和新闻上下文的储能管理数值实验中，该方法展示了改进的经济性能。

Conclusion: LiFT-MPC能够有效利用语言上下文信息改进MPC中的预测质量，提升经济性能，并具有理论上的性能保证。

Abstract: In model predictive control (MPC) with time-varying objectives, predicted signals need to be often incorporated in the cost function, such as prices in energy system operation. These are, however, often difficult to predict from the historical trajectory of these signals alone, as they may depend on other contextual events. We propose LiFT-MPC, an MPC framework that integrates a LiFT (Language-in-the-Loop Feedback Tuning) correction scheme to refine such predictions within the MPC loop. The prediction mechanism is updated online via a control-performance loss function, and we establish a performance guarantee for the resulting closed loop system. Numerical experiments using a realistic example of energy-storage management with real prices and news context to improve predictions, demonstrate an improved economic performance

</details>


### [108] [Variational Formulations for Fractional Integral and Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22659)
*Delfim F. M. Torres*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对分数阶线性方程L^α u = f，定义了合适的对称双线性形式使得分数阶算子L^α关于该形式对称，进而构造分数阶变分泛函，证明该方程的解是该泛函的临界点。对于分数阶积分方程，该双线性形式非退化，所有临界点都是方程的解；对于分数阶微分方程，则与最小二乘法建立了联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为分数阶线性方程建立变分框架，使得分数阶算子在该框架下具有对称性，从而将求解问题转化为寻找某个泛函的临界点问题。

Method: 定义合适的对称双线性形式使分数阶算子L^α关于该形式对称；基于该双线性形式构造分数阶变分泛函；分析该泛函的临界点与给定方程解之间的关系。

Result: 证明了分数阶方程的解是所构造变分泛函的临界点；分数阶积分方程情形下双线性形式非退化，所有临界点都是方程的解；分数阶微分方程情形下与最小二乘法建立了联系。

Conclusion: 成功为分数阶线性方程建立了变分框架，揭示了分数阶方程的解与变分泛函临界点之间的内在联系，并区分了积分方程与微分方程两种情形下的不同结论。

Abstract: Given a fractional-order linear equation $L^αu = f$, we define an appropriate symmetric bilinear form so that the fractional operator $L^α$ is symmetric with respect to that bilinear form. Using the bilinear form, we then define a functional of the fractional calculus of variations proving that the solutions of the given fractional-order equation are critical points of the fractional variational functional. In the case of fractional integral equations, the provided bilinear form is non-degenerate, and all critical points are solutions of the given equation. In the case of fractional differential equations, a relation with the least-squares method is obtained.

</details>


### [109] [Generalized Fine-Tuning of Diffusion Models via Stochastic Control and FBSDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22660)
*Zirui Wang,Lu Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于随机控制视角的扩散模型广义微调框架，引入一般运行成本诱导相对广义路径成本，统一了KL散度和最优传输度量，通过全非线性HJB方程和FBSDE系统刻画最优控制，并揭示了梯度依赖惩罚项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型微调方法多基于熵正则化框架，缺乏统一的理论视角，无法灵活处理多种分布约束下的微调问题。

Method: 从随机控制视角出发，引入一般运行成本函数，推导出全非线性HJB方程；利用FBSDE系统对值函数进行刻画，并在标准正则性条件下证明解的存在唯一性；最优控制呈现由BSDE梯度分量驱动的非线性反馈形式。

Result: 广义微调自然引入了额外的梯度依赖惩罚项，统一了扩散模型在多种分布约束下的微调框架。

Conclusion: 该工作为扩散模型的微调提供了更一般的理论框架，能将KL散度和最优传输等不同度量纳入统一视角，拓宽了扩散模型微调的理论基础。

Abstract: We propose a generalized fine-tuning framework for diffusion models from the perspective of stochastic control. Beyond entropy-regularized formulations, we introduce a general running cost that induces a relative generalized path cost, encompassing both Kullback-Leibler divergence and optimal transport metrics as special cases. This leads to a fully nonlinear Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation. We characterize the value function through a forward-backward stochastic differential equation system and establish existence and uniqueness under standard regularity conditions. The optimal control admits a nonlinear feedback form driven by the backward stochastic differential equation gradient component. Finally, we show that generalized fine-tuning naturally introduces an additional gradient-dependent penalty, providing a unified framework for diffusion fine-tuning under general distributional constraints.

</details>


### [110] [A Nonlinear CTH-Based Adaptive Cruise Controller With Safety and String Stability Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24228)
*Dionysios Theodosis,Amirhossein Samii,Nikolaos Bekiaris-Liberis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对异构车辆队列的纵向控制问题，提出了一种基于恒定车头时距（CTH）策略的非线性自适应巡航控制器。该控制器通过速度相关非线性增益和饱和型非线性，在保证碰撞避免的同时，实现了速度与加速度的有界性，并直接从闭环动力学角度建立了非线性队列稳定性条件，无需线性化或频域分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在车辆队列控制中，保证安全（碰撞避免）和队列稳定性是关键挑战。现有方法多依赖线性化或频域分析，难以处理非线性特性。本文旨在设计一种非线性控制器，在异构车辆队列中同时保证安全性、有界性和队列稳定性。

Method: 基于CTH策略设计非线性自适应巡航控制器；引入速度相关非线性增益保证速度有界；采用饱和型非线性保证加速度有界；定义合适的间距误差变量以得到显式非线性误差动力学；通过正不变集分析保证碰撞自由区域；直接从闭环动力学推导非线性队列稳定性的充分条件。

Result: 证明了碰撞自由区域是正不变的；控制器保证间距误差收敛；建立了针对扰动、前车外部输入和初始条件扰动的L_p队列稳定性条件（1≤p≤∞）。

Conclusion: 提出的非线性控制器能有效解决异构车辆队列的纵向控制问题，在保证安全性的同时实现了队列稳定性，且分析方法避免了线性化或频域假设，具有更强的普适性。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of longitudinal control, with safety and string stability guarantees, in vehicle platoons. A nonlinear adaptive cruise controller based on the Constant Time Headway (CTH) policy is designed for platoons of heterogeneous vehicles, that incorporates a speed-dependent nonlinear gain guaranteeing bounded vehicle speeds and a saturation-type nonlinearity to guarantee bounded (or non-aggressive) accelerations, while preserving collision-avoidance properties of time-headway-based spacing policies. By introducing a suitable spacing-error variable, explicit nonlinear error dynamics are obtained, allowing a direct analysis of the closed-loop system. It is shown that the collision-free region is positively invariant and that the proposed controller guarantees convergence of the spacing errors. Furthermore, nonlinear string-stability properties are established directly from the closed-loop dynamics without resorting to linearization or frequency-domain arguments. In particular, sufficient conditions are derived for $L_p$ string stability, $1\le p\le\infty$, with respect to disturbances, external inputs acting on the leading vehicle, and perturbations of the initial conditions.

</details>


### [111] [The Capacitated $p$-Location Problem with Territorial Coverage Constraint: Efficient Solution and Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22666)
*Felipe Albuquerque,Rosa Figueiredo,Cyrille Genre-Grandpierre*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了带容量约束的p-选址问题（C$p$LP）及其考虑公平性的扩展，提出了带地域覆盖约束的C$p$LP-TC和多尺度变体C$p$LP-MTC，通过强化整数线性规划（ILP）和随机采样空间投票（RSSV）元启发式算法实现高效求解，实验验证了方法的可靠性和公平性约束的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典p-中位问题（$p$MP）和带容量约束的p-选址问题（C$p$LP）缺乏对空间公平性的考量，导致选址方案可能在不同地理单元间产生不公平的服务分配。本研究旨在将公平性约束引入选址优化模型，使设施选址在满足容量限制的同时，兼顾多层级空间单元的公平覆盖。

Method: 1) 提出C$p$LP-TC模型，引入地域覆盖约束以强制空间单元间的公平性；2) 提出C$p$LP-MTC多尺度变体，在嵌套空间层级上同时施加公平性约束；3) 构建带有效不等式的强化ILP公式；4) 开发随机采样空间投票（RSSV）元启发式算法，结合启发式约简阶段与强化ILP。

Result: 在基准实例和真实世界实例上的计算实验表明，所提出的RSSV元启发式算法在效率和可靠性方面表现优异，同时验证了基于地域公平性约束对选址规划公平性的影响。

Conclusion: RSSV方法灵活、参数调优需求低且对非专业用户友好，能够有效求解带公平性约束的容量约束选址问题，为实际选址规划提供了兼顾效率与公平的决策支持工具。

Abstract: This paper studies the Capacitated $p$-Location Problem (C$p$LP) and its extensions incorporating equity considerations. In location science, the $p$-Median problem ($p$MP) is a classical model which selects $p$ facilities from a finite set of candidates to serve a set of customers minimizing total allocation costs. The C$p$LP which includes the Capacitated p-Median Problem (C$p$MP) and its relaxed variant (C$p$MP$^r$), extends the $p$MP by incorporating capacity constraints on facilities.We first introduce the C$p$LP with Territorial Coverage Constraints (C$p$LP-TC), a extension of the CpLP that enforces equity considering the spatial units. Building on this, we propose the multi-scale variant (C$p$LP-MTC), which generalizes territorial coverage constraints across nested spatial scales, allowing equity to be enforced simultaneously at multiple levels of spatial aggregation. A strengthened Integer Linear Programming (ILP) formulation with valid inequalities is presented. In addition, we develop the Random Sampling Spatial Voting (RSSV) matheuristic, which integrates a heuristic reduction phase with the strengthened ILP. The method is flexible, requires minimal parameter tuning, and remains accessible to non specialist users. Computational experiments on benchmark and real world instances demonstrate its efficiency and reliability. The results also demonstrate the impact of equity territorial based constraints on fairness in location planning.

</details>


### [112] [Kernel-Type Fractional Extensions of Reverse Callebaut, Rogers Hölder and Cauchy Schwarz Inequalities on Time Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22670)
*Nimai Sarkar,Gobinda Ghosh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在时间尺度上建立了基于核函数的分数阶反向Callebaut、Rogers-Hölder和Cauchy-Schwarz不等式，通过diamond-α积分和非负核算子引入记忆效应，统一了连续、离散、量子及分数阶动态情形下的反向不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反向不等式在连续、离散和量子情形下分别研究，缺乏统一框架；同时经典不等式未考虑记忆效应。本文旨在建立包含核函数的分数阶扩展，统一各类动态情形下的反向不等式。

Method: 利用时间尺度上的diamond-α积分和非负核算子，构造核型分数阶扩展形式；通过选取特定核函数和时间尺度，将结果退化为经典对应情形；当核取为单位核时，恢复经典diamond-α动态不等式。

Result: 建立了核型分数阶反向Callebaut、Rogers-Hölder和Cauchy-Schwarz不等式；推导出分数阶反向Cauchy-Schwarz不等式和加权反向Rogers-Hölder不等式等特例；结果统一了连续、离散、量子及分数阶动态情形。

Conclusion: 提供了一种研究反向不等式的统一方法，可应用于Volterra型方程和时滞动态方程的先验估计。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish kernel-type fractional extensions of reverse Callebaut, Rogers--Hölder and Cauchy--Schwarz inequalities on time scales. The proposed results are formulated by means of a nonnegative kernel operator associated with the diamond-$α$ integral, which enables the inclusion of memory effects within the framework of dynamic inequalities. By choosing particular kernels and time scales, the obtained inequalities reduce to their continuous, discrete and quantum counterparts. Moreover, when the kernel is taken as the identity kernel, the results recover the corresponding classical diamond-$α$ dynamic inequalities. Several consequences are derived as special cases, including fractional reverse Cauchy--Schwarz inequalities and weighted reverse Rogers--Hölder inequalities. The results provide a unified approach for studying reverse inequalities in continuous, discrete, quantum and fractional dynamic settings and may be applied to obtain a priori estimates for Volterra-type and delay dynamic equations.

</details>


### [113] [A QUBO-Based Optimization Framework for ATM Cash Replenishment Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22675)
*Riccardo Aiolfi,Giulia Montani,Valeria Zaffaroni,Luca Toffanetti,Davide Veronelli,Francesca Cibrario,Davide Caputo,Davide Corbelletto*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于二次无约束二元优化（QUBO）模型的ATM现金补货调度方法，利用GPU加速求解器MegaQUBO进行求解。在意大利276台ATM的真实数据集上测试，相比阈值策略，该方法实现了约15%-18%的成本降低，同时保持了约99.8%-99.9%的高服务水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ATM网络中的现金补货管理需要在不确定且时变的取款需求下，安排补货计划以最小化运营成本，同时维持高服务水平并避免现金耗尽，这是一个具有重要实际意义的优化问题。

Method: 将ATM现金补货问题建模为QUBO模型，通过惩罚项纳入运营约束，目标函数结合了固定和可变补货成本、同址折扣以及延迟补货导致服务中断的惩罚。使用GPU加速的QUBO求解器MegaQUBO进行求解。

Result: 在意大利276台ATM的真实数据集（2022年4月、5月、10月和11月）上，所提方法相比阈值策略一致实现了约15%-18%的成本降低，平均服务水平维持在约99.8%-99.9%。

Conclusion: 基于QUBO的优化方法结合GPU加速求解，能够为大规模ATM现金物流提供可实际部署的决策支持工具。

Abstract: The management of cash replenishment in Automated Teller Machine (ATM) networks requires scheduling recharges in order to minimize operational costs while maintaining high service levels and avoiding cash-outs, under uncertain and time-varying withdrawal demand. This work formulates the ATM cash replenishment problem through a Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) model, which naturally captures nonlinear cost interactions, while incorporating operational constraints through penalty terms. The objective function combines fixed and variable replenishment costs with co-location discounts, as well as penalties for a late replenishment that could cause a service interruption. The resulting QUBO instances are solved using MegaQUBO, a GPU-accelerated QUBO solver. An empirical evaluation on a real dataset of 276 ATMs located in Italy, covering four representative months of 2022 (April, May, October, and November), benchmarks the proposed approach against a threshold-based operational policy. Results show consistent cost reductions of approximately 15%-18% while maintaining an excellent average service level (around 99.8%-99.9). Overall, the study demonstrates that QUBO-based optimization, coupled with GPU-based solving, can provide a practically deployable decision-support tool for large-scale ATM cash logistics.

</details>


### [114] [Convexifying Mean-Field Control: An Occupation-Measure and Frank-Wolfe Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22678)
*Di Yu,Sixiong You,Chaoying Pei*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于占用测度优化的平均场控制（MFC）框架，利用Frank-Wolfe算法在测度空间求解凸松弛后的最优控制问题，避免了状态空间离散化，在保持O(1/k)收敛速率的同时自然融入交互和安全约束，数值实验表明该方法在二维下与解析/PDE基线一致，且能高效扩展到含多个障碍的三维环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模机器人集群需要平均场控制方法，但经典PDE方法在高维计算困难，而机器学习方法虽可扩展但牺牲了近似保证。本文旨在建立一种兼具计算可扩展性与理论保证的优化框架。

Method: 将MFC问题提升到占用测度空间，形成测度上的凸优化问题；采用Frank-Wolfe算法在测度空间迭代求解，每次迭代简化为一个可处理的最优控制子问题。

Result: 该方法在二维场景下与解析解和PDE基线吻合；可扩展到含多个障碍物的三维环境，标准工作站上十分钟内即可求解包含十个障碍物的完整三维实例，而经典网格PDE求解器此时已不可行。

Conclusion: 所提框架兼具理论收敛保证（O(1/k)）与实用可扩展性，避免了状态空间离散化，能自然处理交互与安全约束，为高维大规模机器人集群控制提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: Large-scale robotic swarms motivate the use of mean-field control (MFC). Classical partial differential equation (PDE)-based formulations provide a principled framework but can become computationally challenging in higher dimensions, whereas machine learning achieves scalability at the cost of approximation and guarantees. In this work, we establish an optimization-based framework that lifts the MFC problem into the space of occupation measures, resulting in a convex relaxation formulated as an optimization over measures. The resulting problem is solved using a Frank-Wolfe (FW) algorithm in the measure space, with each iteration reduced to a tractable optimal control problem. This approach retains the O(1/k) convergence rate of FW, avoids discretization of the state space, and naturally incorporates interaction and safety constraints. Numerical experiments demonstrate agreement with analytic and PDE-based baselines in two dimensions and show that the method scales to three-dimensional environments with multiple obstacles, where standard grid-based PDE solvers become impractical. A full 3D instance with ten obstacles is solved in minutes on a standard workstation, underscoring the practicality and scalability of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [115] [H$_1$-ISS Analysis and Boundary Control Design for Coupled Linear ODEs and Hyperbolic PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22881)
*Franthiescolly Vieira de Carvalho,Daniel Coutinho,Gustavo de Andrade,Christophe Prieur,Mohamed Maghenem*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对边界控制器设计问题，研究了耦合线性ODE与齐次线性双曲PDE系统的输入-状态稳定性（ISS）与镇定问题，将已有的Lyapunov-based ISS条件推广到H₁空间，并基于线性矩阵不等式给出了稳定性分析与控制设计条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决分布式参数系统中边界控制器的设计问题，实现闭环系统的渐近稳定性和对外部扰动的衰减，特别是针对耦合线性ODE与齐次线性双曲PDE系统，处理位于边界的驱动、传感和扰动输入。

Method: 将已有的Lyapunov-based ISS条件从原有系统类推广到H₁设置下，放宽对扰动输入的假设条件，确保系统状态H₁范数有界并给出上界；进一步将理论结果转化为基于线性矩阵不等式（LMI）的稳定性分析与控制设计条件。

Result: 在H₁范数意义下证明了非消失扰动与相容初始条件下系统状态的有界性，并给出了H₁范数的上界；通过数值算例验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 成功将ISS分析框架推广至更广泛的一类耦合ODE-PDE边界控制系统，并提供了可计算的LMI形式的设计条件，为实际工程应用提供了理论基础和工具。

Abstract: This paper considers the general problem of the design of boundary controllers for distributed parameter systems. The control objectives are the asymptotic stability of the closed-loop system, as well as the disturbance attenuation of exogenous disturbances affecting the measurements and boundary conditions. More specifically, this work studies input-to-state stability (ISS) and stabilization for systems governed by coupled linear ordinary differential equations (ODEs) and homogeneous linear hyperbolic partial differential equations (PDEs), where actuation, sensing, and disturbance inputs are located at the system boundaries. First, we extend a previously established Lyapunov-based ISS condition for a similar class of systems to the H$_1$ setting under mild assumptions on the disturbance inputs. This extension not only ensures that the H$_1$-norm of the system states driven by non-vanishing disturbances and compatible initial conditions are bounded, but also provides an upper bound on the system H$_1$-norm. Subsequently, these theoretical results are applied to derive stability analysis and control design conditions expressed in terms of linear matrix inequalities. Numerical examples are provided to illustrate the effectiveness and potential of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [116] [Feedback stopping rules in path-dependent controller-stopper games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22882)
*Magnus Perninge*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究有限时域、零和的控制器-停止者博弈，其中停止者观察状态过程并实施反馈停止规则。基于非线性Snell包络表示，通过扩展首次接触原理，本文证明该博弈存在价值。该方法纯概率化，适用于路径依赖系统且允许SDE退化。作为应用，零和博弈的最优反馈停止规则可作为ε>0时非零和博弈ε-纳什均衡的组成部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 严格证明零和控制器-停止者博弈存在价值，并为非零和博弈提供均衡构建基础。

Method: 基于非线性Snell包络表示，扩展首次接触原理，采用纯概率化方法处理路径依赖系统及退化SDE。

Result: 证明了该博弈存在价值，并得到了最优反馈停止规则。

Conclusion: 零和博弈的最优反馈停止规则可作为非零和博弈ε-纳什均衡的组成部分，为相关博弈提供理论工具。

Abstract: We investigate finite-horizon, zero-sum controller-stopper games in which the stopper observes the state process and implements a feedback stopping rule. Building on a nonlinear Snell envelope representation for a related game established in a companion paper, we prove that our game admits a value by extending the associated first-contact principle. Our approach is purely probabilistic and yields an optimal feedback stopping rule for path-dependent systems while allowing for degeneracy in the underlying stochastic differential equation (SDE).
  As an application, we consider nonzero-sum controller-stopper games and show that the optimal feedback stopping rule for a zero-sum game appears as a component of a $\varepsilon$-Nash equilibrium for every $\varepsilon>0$.

</details>


### [117] [Curvature Recycling Douglas-Rachford Splitting: Transported Quasi-Newton Models for Expensive Smooth Proximal Subproblems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22895)
*Musa Maharramov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出曲率回收Douglas-Rachford分裂（CR-DRS）方法，通过传输旧残差、重用拟牛顿模型和移动旧近端状态来减少光滑近端子问题求解中昂贵的梯度调用。在强凸性和光滑性条件下，小增益条件保证全局线性收敛，传输的Dennis-Moré条件实现局部超线性收敛。ℓ1和全变分正则化实验表明，相比重启和仅曲率拟牛顿方法，CR-DRS显著减少了昂贵梯度调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Douglas-Rachford分裂（DRS）求解光滑+非光滑复合优化问题时，需要求解一系列光滑近端子问题。这些子问题中心不同但共享相同的非线性曲率，传统方法（如重启或仅使用曲率信息）未能充分利用这一结构，导致每次外迭代都需多次评估昂贵的梯度。本文旨在利用子问题间的曲率共享特性，通过回收和传输历史信息来减少梯度调用次数。

Method: 提出CR-DRS方法，包含三个关键技术：(1) 将旧精确残差传输到新的近端中心；(2) 重用拟牛顿模型（直接/逆BFGS和受保护的SR1实现）；(3) 在评估第一个新梯度之前移动旧近端状态。提供认证变体：仅当传输的拟牛顿步通过下降测试和残差测试时才被接受，否则执行固定次数的安全梯度步。理论分析建立小增益条件保证全局线性收敛，传输的Dennis-Moré条件实现局部超线性收敛，并推导了全模型和活动子空间充分条件。

Result: 在ℓ1正则化和全变分正则化问题上的实验表明，CR-DRS相比重启拟牛顿方法和仅曲率拟牛顿近端求解方法，大幅减少了昂贵梯度调用的次数。对于直接近端ℓ1模型，还与FISTA进行了比较，展示了竞争力。

Conclusion: CR-DRS通过有效利用DRS子问题间的曲率共享特性，将历史信息（残差、拟牛顿模型、近端状态）在子问题间传输，在保证理论收敛性的同时显著降低了梯度评估成本，为求解光滑+非光滑复合优化问题提供了一种高效方法。

Abstract: We consider \[
  \min_x f(x)+g(x), \] where $f$ is smooth but its value and gradient are expensive, while $g$ is nonsmooth and has a cheap proximal map. Douglas--Rachford splitting (DRS) then requires a sequence of smooth proximal solves. These solves have different centers but share the same nonlinear curvature.
  We introduce curvature-recycling DRS (CR-DRS). The method transports an old exact residual to the new proximal center, reuses a quasi-Newton model, and moves the old proximal state before the first new gradient is evaluated. We give paired direct and inverse BFGS and safeguarded symmetric-rank-one realizations. A certified variant accepts a transported quasi-Newton step only when it passes descent and residual tests; otherwise it uses a fixed number of safe gradient steps. Under strong convexity and smoothness, a small-gain condition gives global linear convergence with a constant number of new gradients per outer iteration. A transported Dennis--Moré condition gives local superlinear reduction of the new proximal error. We show that this condition is not automatic, derive full-model and active-subspace sufficient conditions, and relate the active subspace to the local DRS dynamics induced by the nonsmooth proximal map.
  Experiments on $\ell_1$- and total-variation-regularized problems show large reductions in expensive-gradient calls relative to restarted and curvature-only quasi-Newton proximal solves. For directly proximal $\ell_1$ models, we also compare with FISTA.

</details>


### [118] [Nesterov acceleration in optimizing over probability measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23008)
*Jiaqi Tang,Qin Li,Wilfrid Gangbo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对概率测度空间上的优化问题，提出了Heavy-ball和Nesterov加速方法，建立了与欧几里得空间匹配的非渐近收敛保证，并通过两种互补的提升程序（哈密顿提升和希尔伯特空间提升）克服了动量定义和数值实现的困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率测度优化在现代机器学习、科学计算和不确定性量化中日益重要，但现有方法缺乏加速收敛机制，亟需将Nesterov加速等经典技术从欧几里得空间推广到概率测度空间。

Method: 提出两种互补的提升程序：1) 通过哈密顿 formulering 将概率测度提升到相空间，引入动量变量；2) 将概率测度提升到公共希尔伯特空间，恢复线性结构以利于收敛分析，同时得到可执行的粒子动力学。

Result: 建立了与欧几里得空间Heavy-ball和Nesterov加速方法相匹配的非渐近收敛率，同时考虑了迭代次数和粒子数量的影响。

Conclusion: 两种互补的提升程序为概率测度空间上基于动量的加速优化方法的设计、分析和实现提供了系统方法论。

Abstract: Optimization over probability measures has become an increasingly important paradigm in modern machine learning, scientific computing, and uncertainty quantification. Motivated by Nesterov's accelerated gradient method in Euclidean space, we develop Heavy-ball and Nesterov acceleration methods over the probability measure space $\mathcal{P}_2$ and establish non-asymptotic convergence guarantees that match their Euclidean counterparts. In particular, we derive convergence rates with respect to both the number of iterations and the number of particles used to represent the underlying probability distributions.
  Extending accelerated optimization from Euclidean space to probability measures is challenging. The natural notion of momentum requires concepts such as tangent bundles of the set of probability space and they are hard to operate numerically. To overcome these difficulties, we introduce two complementary lifting procedures. The first lifts probability measures to phase space through a Hamiltonian formulation, introducing momentum variables into the dynamics. The second lifts probability measures to a common Hilbert space, restoring the linear structure required for convergence analysis while simultaneously yielding executable particle dynamics. Together, these two complementary lifting procedures provide a systematic methodology for designing, analyzing, and implementing momentum-based accelerated optimization methods over probability measure spaces.

</details>


### [119] [Output Regulation for Linear Parabolic Systems Using Finite-Dimensional Tracking-Error-Based Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23032)
*Bingsen Li,Emilia Fridman,George Weiss*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对1-D扩散反应系统的输出调节问题，提出了一种基于有限维跟踪误差的调节器设计方法。通过模态分解、全阶观测器设计和LMI条件，将输出调节问题转化为部分镇定问题，并分析了时变测量延迟下的系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决不稳定外部系统驱动的1-D扩散反应系统的输出调节问题，现有方法在处理此类耦合系统时存在局限，需要一种构造性的设计方法。

Method: 建立了调节方程推导组合plant；利用模态分解方法刻画截断模式的可观测性；设计全阶观测器（非标准镇定中的仅依赖不稳定模式）；基于LMI确定观测器维度并证明其可行性。

Result: 证明了观测器增益范数相对于观测器维度一致有界；给出了LMI条件用于确定观测器维度并证明大维度下LMI可行；分析了时变测量延迟下的输出调节问题。

Conclusion: 成功将输出调节问题转化为部分镇定问题，设计了有限维跟踪误差调节器，理论结果得到了数值验证。

Abstract: This paper addresses the output regulation problem for 1-D diffusion-reaction system, where both the disturbance and reference signals are generated by an unstable exosystem. We propose a constructive approach to the design of a finite-dimensional tracking error-based regulator for this class of possibly unstable systems. Based on the regulator equations, a combined plant is derived, converting the output regulation problem into a partial stabilization problem for the combined system. Using the modal decomposition method, the observability of the truncated modes is characterized under appropriate transmission zeros and observability conditions. For the controller design, unlike in the standard stabilization case, where the observer gain is dependent on the unstable modes only, the observer gain here has full order due to the coupling introduced by the exosystem. We prove that the observer gain can be designed such that its norm remains uniformly bounded with respect to the dimension of the observer. LMI-based conditions are provided for determining the observer dimension, and it is shown that the LMI is feasible for a sufficiently large dimension. Finally, the output regulation problem in the presence of unknown time-varying measurement delays is analyzed. Numerical examples are provided to validate the theoretical results.

</details>


### [120] [Optimization of Kinetic--Stoichiometric Growth Bounds over Autocatalytic Subnetworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23107)
*Víctor Blanco,Gabriel González*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合整数双线性优化模型，用于在化学反应系统中选择最大化生长界（动力学-化学计量平衡增长界）的自催化子网络，并开发了精确参数算法，实现了全局最优解的有限收敛。实验表明，单纯最大化结构放大因子（MAF）并不总能保证最佳生长潜力，必须同时考虑反应动力学效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化方法通过最大化结构放大因子（MAF）来识别自催化子网络，但结构放大本身并不能确定生长潜力，因为高放大的子网络可能动力学效率低下。作者受近期关于可扩展动力学下平衡生长的动力学-化学计量界启发，旨在解决选择最大化生长界的自催化子网络问题。

Method: 将问题建模为混合整数双线性优化模型，结合超图选择与广义分数放大约束；利用动力学范数的离散结构，开发了一种精确参数算法，每个子问题简化为MAF最大化，并通过Dinkelbach型广义分数规划方法求解；建立了有限收敛性和全局最优性。

Result: 在Oregonator、formose反应、碳固定循环和合成基准网络上应用，表明最大化动力学-化学计量界与单独最大化MAF会产生不同结果，揭示了化学计量放大、反应动力学与平衡生长之间的相互作用。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种精确优化方法论，用于识别具有最高理论生长潜力的反应子网络，为自催化网络设计提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Autocatalytic subnetworks govern sustained production and balanced growth in chemical reaction systems and can be naturally represented as directed multihypergraphs that jointly encode stoichiometric structure and reaction kinetics. Existing optimization approaches identify such subnetworks by maximizing their structural amplification, typically measured through the Maximum Amplification Factor (MAF). However, structural amplification alone does not determine growth potential, since highly amplifying subnetworks may be kinetically inefficient. Motivated by recent kinetic--stoichiometric bounds on balanced growth under scalable dynamics, we study the problem of selecting the autocatalytic subnetwork maximizing the growth bound $(α^*-1)\|\mathbb{S}|_{\mathcal A'}\|_κ$, where $α^*$ is the MAF and $\|\mathbb{S}|_{\mathcal A'}\|_κ$ is a kinetic consumption norm. We formulate the problem as a mixed-integer bilinear optimization model combining hypergraph selection with generalized fractional amplification constraints. Exploiting the discrete structure of the kinetic norm, we develop an exact parametric algorithm in which each subproblem reduces to MAF maximization and is solved through a Dinkelbach-type generalized fractional programming method. Finite convergence and global optimality are established. Applications to the Oregonator, the formose reaction, carbon-fixation cycles, and synthetic benchmark networks show when maximizing the kinetic--stoichiometric bound differs from maximizing the MAF alone, revealing the interplay between stoichiometric amplification, reaction kinetics, and balanced growth. The proposed framework provides an exact optimization methodology for identifying reaction subnetworks with the highest theoretical growth potential.

</details>


### [121] [A Novel Optimization Model for Solving the Timetabling Problem at Finnish Universities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23109)
*Kimmo Nurmi,Jari Kyngäs,Pauliina Halme*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了与芬兰三所大学及教育管理系统提供商合作的排课优化项目，提出了CRONOPT模型，旨在同时优化大学资源利用、教师工作负荷与学生学业进度三个目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 芬兰大学面临排课问题的新挑战，需要平衡大学、教师和学生三方利益相关者的偏好；现有方法无法满足新的实践需求，存在研究空白。

Method: 项目分为可行性研究、准备和实施三个阶段；提出了CRONOPT模型，用于同时优化三个目标：有效利用大学资源、教师工作量与福祉、学生学业顺利推进；优化方案直接集成到芬兰大学主流教育管理系统中。

Result: 在可行性研究阶段识别出多个研究空白和新颖的实践目标；开发了CRONOPT优化模型，并与主流教育管理系统完成集成。

Conclusion: 该合作项目旨在实现全新的排课规划流程管理方式，而不仅仅是简化排课创建过程；CRONOPT模型为平衡多方利益提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We study a timetabling problem faced by Finnish universities, where the challenge is to balance the preferences of three key stakeholders: the university, teachers, and students. Educational timetabling is an essential task that ensures that everything runs effortlessly and collaboratively. Its efficiency significantly impacts the smooth operation of the institution's educational programs and its financial success. As universities have evolved and competition among them has intensified, the features of the university timetabling problem have changed. This paper describes an ongoing optimization project with three Finnish universities and a leading provider of educational management systems for universities. The main goal of this cooperation is not merely to streamline the creation of timetables, but to enable an entirely new approach to manage the entire planning process. The project consists of three six-month phases: the feasibility study, the preparation phase, and the implementation. During the feasibility study, several research-related gaps and novel practical goals were identified. To address these gaps and goals, this paper presents a unique CRONOPT model for tackling three targets simultaneously: effective use of university resources, teacher workload and well-being, and smooth progression of students' studies. The optimization solution developed through the project integrates directly with the market-leading educational management system for Finnish universities.

</details>


### [122] [KAYROS: An Anytime and Exact Open-Source Solver for Duration-Minimization Time-Dependent Vehicle Routing. A Technical Report and a Case Study in Human-AI Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23116)
*Florian Rascoussier*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: KAYROS是一款开源求解器，用于最小化行程时间的时变车辆路径问题（TDVRPTW/TDVRP），支持任意时间输出渐近最优解并证明最优性，基于分段线性旅行时间函数而非时间离散化。它在MAMUT基准上发布了468个最优性证明，并引入了包含1080个实例的Poryos2026基准族。论文还介绍了实现过程中的人机协作模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时变车辆路径问题在现实中普遍存在（如交通拥堵），但此前缺乏既支持任意时间输出渐近最优解又能证明最优性的开源求解器。现有方法或依赖时间离散化（精度损失），或存在专有依赖，或无法提供可公开验证的最优性证明。

Method: 基于分支定价切割（branch-price-and-cut）框架，扩展了Lera-Romero等人的开源求解器，引入：开放LP后端、任意时间与热启动行为、校验器精确定价、分段线性旅行时间的精确处理（包括步进式旅行时间）。

Result: 在MAMUT基准上发布了468个最优性证明（每个需四个独立求解器一致），其中5个严格优于已发表的参考值。生成了Poryos2026基准族（1080个实例），每个实例均附带校验器验证的最佳已知解。

Conclusion: KAYROS是首个同时支持任意时间输出和最优性证明的开源时变VRP求解器，Poryos2026为后续研究提供了标准化基准。论文也展示了人机协作如何保障复杂研究工作的可验证性。

Abstract: KAYROS is an open-source solver for duration-minimization time-dependent vehicle routing problems, with or without time windows (TDVRPTW, TDVRP). In these variants, travel times change with departure time, and each route's dispatch time is a decision. To the best of the author's knowledge, it is the first openly available solver that is both anytime, streaming improving solutions from the first seconds, and exact, proving optimality with publicly verifiable certificates, for these problems over rich piecewise-linear travel- time functions rather than a time discretization. It has no proprietary dependency and installs with one command. It builds on the state of the art for time-dependent function composition and exact solving, extending the open-source branch-price-and-cut solver of Lera-Romero, Miranda Bront and Soulignac (2020) with an open LP backend, anytime and warm- start behavior, checker-exact pricing, and exact treatment of stepwise travel times. On the MAMUT-routing benchmark collection, KAYROS stands behind 468 published optimality certificates, each requiring agreement among four independent solves, and five certificates strictly improve published reference values. The report also introduces Poryos2026, a benchmark family designed and generated by the author from real OpenStreetMap city road networks. Its 1,080 paired CVRP, VRPTW, TDVRP and TDVRPTW instances combine real road geometries with controlled synthetic demands, time windows and congestion. Every instance carries a checker-validated best-known solution. This report presents the solver, its certification protocol, the benchmark's generation and feasibility guarantees, and their experimental connection for a broad technical audience. It is also a case study in the intensive human-AI collaboration that made this body of work feasible while keeping its claims independently verifiable.

</details>


### [123] [Support profiles of full-capacity Pareto spectra of order three](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23320)
*Samir Adly*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了3阶实矩阵中Pareto特征值的最大数目（9个）的分布规律，证明了对于每种支持集选择，大小为1、2、3的支持集所分配的特征值数量必须为(1,5,3)、(2,4,3)或(2,5,2)三种配置之一，并给出了每种配置的显式满容量矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知3阶实矩阵最多有9个不同的Pareto特征值，但尚不清楚达到该最大值时这些特征值如何由其支持集（support）产生，以及不同大小支持集之间的数量分布规律。

Method: 利用2阶问题的界值以及对单点支持集、配对支持集和全支持集的多种限制条件进行推导；特别地，通过证明产生2个特征值的配对支持集所构成的图中不含三角形这一关键结论，结合组合计数论证得到三种可能的配置。

Result: 证明了达到9个Pareto特征值时，支持集大小1、2、3对应的特征值数量必须为(1,5,3)、(2,4,3)或(2,5,2)之一；给出了每种配置的显式满容量矩阵，并证明了每种配置在Euclidean开集中非空，其中每个Pareto特征值有唯一产生支持集。

Conclusion: 完整刻画了3阶矩阵达到最大Pareto特征值数目时支持集的分布结构，并提供了构造性示例。

Abstract: For a given real matrix $A\in\R^{n\times n}$ of order $n\geq 1$, a Pareto eigenvalue is a scalar $λ\in\R$ for which there exists a nonzero vector $x\in\R^n_+$ such that $Ax-λx\in\R^n_+$ and $\langle x,Ax-λx\rangle=0$. It is knwon that a real matrix of order $n=3$ can have at most $9$ distinct Pareto eigenvalues. We study the matrices for which this maximal number is attained and classify the way in which the $9$ Pareto eigenvalues are produced by their supports.
  A Pareto eigenvalue may be produced by more than one support. We therefore choose one producing support for each distinct values. The main contribution of this paper is to prove that, for every such choice, the numbers of values assigned to supports of sizes $1$, $2$, and $3$ are necessarily $$
  (1,5,3),\qquad (2,4,3),\qquad\text{or}\qquad (2,5,2). $$ The proof uses bounds from the order $n=2$ problem and several restrictions on singleton, pair, and full supports. In particular, the graph formed by the pair supports producing $2$ values contains no triangle. These arguments also gives anohter proof that the maximum Pareto capacity in order $3$ is equal to $9$.
  For each of the $3$ profiles, we give an explicit full-capacity matrix with $9$ regular Pareto eigenvalues. We also prove that each profile occurs on a nonempty Euclidean-open set where every Pareto eigenvalue has a unique producing support.

</details>


### [124] [New Globalized Newton-Type Methods for Nonconvex Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23433)
*Vo Thanh Phat,Tuyen Tran*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种避免重复Hessian正则化的通用线搜索Newton框架，在非凸优化中利用Newton方向（仅当良定义且合适时），覆盖多种混合梯度-Newton方法并导出新extragradient Newton法，在PLK条件下证明全局收敛及局部超线性/二次收敛，首次应用于强拟凸优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全局收敛的Newton型方法通常要求目标函数凸或强凸，而非凸优化方法往往需要每轮Hessian正则化，缺乏一个避免重复正则化且保持全局收敛的通用框架。

Method: 提出通用线搜索Newton框架，仅在Newton方向良定义且合适时使用，否则回退到梯度方向，并引入新extragradient Newton变体。在PLK条件下建立全局收敛，在正则性假设下证明局部超线性/二次收敛。

Result: 新框架覆盖若干现有混合梯度-Newton方法，首次为强拟凸优化提供Newton型算法及完整收敛分析，数值实验验证有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架有效避免了非凸优化中重复Hessian正则化的需求，同时保证了全局收敛和快速局部收敛，并拓展到强拟凸优化这一重要非凸问题类别。

Abstract: Newton's method is one of the most effective second-order algorithms for smooth optimization because of its fast local convergence. However, existing globally convergent Newton-type methods typically require convexity or strong convexity of the objective function, while approaches for nonconvex optimization often rely on Hessian regularization at every iteration. In this paper, we propose a general line-search Newton framework for unconstrained optimization that avoids repeated Hessian regularization by exploiting the Newton direction only when it is well-defined and suitable. The proposed framework encompasses several existing hybrid gradient--Newton methods as special cases and naturally yields a new extragradient Newton method. We establish global convergence under mild assumptions, including the Polyak--Lojasiewicz--Kurdyka (PLK) condition, allowing both isolated and nonisolated accumulation points. We further prove local superlinear and quadratic convergence under appropriate regularity assumptions. Finally, we apply the proposed framework to strongly quasiconvex optimization and provide, to the best of our knowledge, the first Newton-type algorithm together with a comprehensive convergence analysis for this important class of nonconvex optimization problems. Numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [125] [On the Bellman equation in recursive stochastic dynamic programming with the CES aggregator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23475)
*Anna Jaśkiewicz,Andrzej S. Nowak*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有CES聚合器和熵风险度量确定性等价算子的无限时域马尔可夫决策过程，证明了Bellman方程解的存在唯一性，并表明Bellman方程的可测最大值选择子构成最优平稳策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为具有递归效用（CES聚合器与负熵风险度量）的无限时域MDP建立严格的数学理论基础，特别是Bellman方程解的存在唯一性及最优策略的存在性。

Method: 在原始数据的温和条件下，通过不动点论证证明Bellman方程解的存在唯一性；证明任意平稳政策下Koopmans方程解的唯一性；将方法推广到其他确定性等价算子（包括风险中性情形）。

Result: 1) Bellman方程存在唯一解；2) 任意平稳政策对应的Koopmans方程存在唯一解；3) Bellman方程的可测最大值选择子是最优平稳策略。

Conclusion: 所建立的框架为递归效用下的动态规划问题提供了坚实的理论基础，且方法可推广至其他确定性等价算子。
